Hash the value to get a 64-bit hash\n2. Use the first `n_bits` to determine the bucket/register index\n3. Find the position of the leftmost 1 in the remaining bits (rho function)\n4. i >= 0; i--) {\n    if (bset.test(i)) {\n      // Position from left: (BITSET_CAPACITY - 1 - i) + 1 = BITSET_CAPACITY - i\n      return static_cast<uint64_t>(BITSET_CAPACITY - i);\n    }\n  }\n  // If no 1 found, return position after the last bit\n  return static_cast<uint64_t>(BITSET_CAPACITY + 1);\n}\n\n/**\n * @brief Adds a value into the HyperLogLog.\n *\n * @param[in] val - value that's added into hyperloglog\n */\ntemplate <typename KeyType>\nauto HyperLogLog<KeyType>::AddElem(KeyType val) -> void {\n  if (n_bits_ <= 0) {\n    return;\n  }\n\n  std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);\n\n  // Calculate the hash of the value\n  hash_t hash = CalculateHash(val);\n  std::bitset<BITSET_CAPACITY> binary = ComputeBinary(hash);\n\n  // Extract the bucket index from the FIRST n_bits (MSB side)\n  // Bucket index is determined by the top n_bits_ of the hash\n  size_t bucket_idx = 0;\n  for (int i = 0; i < n_bits_; i++) {\n    if (binary.test(BITSET_CAPACITY - 1 - i)) {\n      bucket_idx |= (1ULL << (n_bits_ - 1 - i));\n    }\n  }\n\n  // Create a new bitset with only the remaining bits (after removing top n_bits_)\n  // Set top n_bits_ to 0\n  std::bitset<BITSET_CAPACITY> remaining_bits = binary;\n  for (int i = 0; i < n_bits_; i++) {\n    remaining_bits.reset(BITSET_CAPACITY - 1 - i);\n  }\n\n  // Find position of leftmost 1 in the remaining bits\n  uint64_t position = PositionOfLeftmostOne(remaining_bits);\n\n  // Update register if this position is larger\n  if (position > registers_[bucket_idx]) {\n    registers_[bucket_idx] = position;\n  }\n}\n\n/**\n * @brief Function that computes cardinality.\n */\ntemplate <typename KeyType>\nauto HyperLogLog<KeyType>::ComputeCardinality() -> void {\n  if (n_bits_ <= 0 || registers_.empty()) {\n    cardinality_ = 0;\n    return;\n  }\n\n  // Number of buckets\n  size_t m = registers_.size();\n\n  // Compute the harmonic mean: sum of 2^(-M[j])\n  double sum = 0.0;\n  for (size_t j = 0; j < m; j++) {\n    sum += std::pow(2.0, -static_cast<double>(registers_[j]));\n  }\n\n  // Cardinality = CONSTANT * m^2 / sum\n  double raw_estimate = CONSTANT * static_cast<double>(m) * static_cast<double>(m) / sum;\n\n  cardinality_ = static_cast<size_t>(raw_estimate);\n}\n\ntemplate class HyperLogLog<int64_t>;\ntemplate class HyperLogLog<std::string>;\n\n}  // namespace bustub\n"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":0,"cache_creation_input_tokens":276,"cache_read_input_tokens":56861,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":276,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":8,"service_tier":"standard"},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"4b8426ba-0733-4a97-add4-4f00343c33e5","uuid":"bd5c56f1-85ff-4056-bba0-bb824633b4f9"}

 // Mark as invalid\n    dense_bucket_.resize(0);\n  } else {\n    // Number of buckets is 2^n_leading_bits\n    size_t num_buckets = 1ULL << n_leading_bits_;\n    dense_bucket_.resize(num_buckets, std::bitset<DENSE_BUCKET_SIZE>(0));\n  }\n}\n\n/** @brief Element is added for HLL calculation. */\ntemplate <typename KeyType>\nauto HyperLogLogPresto<KeyType>::AddElem(KeyType val) -> void {\n  if (n_leading_bits_ < 0) {\n    return;\n  }\n\n  std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);\n\n  // Get hash value\n  hash_t hash = CalculateHash(val);\n\n  // Extract bucket index from the FIRST n_leading_bits (MSB side)\n  // For a 64-bit hash, the top bits determine the bucket\n  uint16_t bucket_idx = 0;\n  if (n_leading_bits_ > 0) {\n    bucket_idx = static_cast<uint16_t>(hash >> (64 - n_leading_bits_));\n  }\n\n  // Calculate position of leftmost 1 in the remaining bits\n  // Clear the top n_leading_bits_ from the hash\n  hash_t remaining;\n  if (n_leading_bits_ >= 64) {\n    remaining = 0;\n  } else {\n    // Mask out the top n_leading_bits_ bits\n    if (n_leading_bits_ == 0) {\n      remaining = hash;\n    } else {\n      remaining = hash << n_leading_bits_;\n    }\n  }\n\n  // Count leading zeros + 1 to get position of leftmost 1\n  uint64_t position;\n  if (remaining == 0) {\n    // All zeros, position is at the end\n    position = 64 - n_leading_bits_ + 1;\n  } else {\n    // Find position of leftmost 1 (1-indexed)\n    position = 1;\n    for (int i = 63; i >= 0; i--) {\n      if ((remaining >> i) & 1) {\n        break;\n      }\n      position++;\n    }\n  }\n\n  // Now store this value using dense_bucket_ (4 bits) and overflow_bucket_ (3 bits)\n  // Total 7 bits can represent values 0-127\n  // Dense bucket stores lower 4 bits\n  // Overflow bucket stores upper 3 bits\n\n  // Current value in registers\n  uint64_t current_dense = dense_bucket_[bucket_idx].to_ullong();\n  uint64_t current_overflow = 0;\n  if (overflow_bucket_.find(bucket_idx) != overflow_bucket_.end()) {\n    current_overflow = overflow_bucket_[bucket_idx].to_ullong();\n  }\n  uint64_t current_value = (current_overflow << DENSE_BUCKET_SIZE) | current_dense;\n\n  // Update if new position is larger\n  if (position > current_value) {\n    // Store new value\n    uint64_t new_dense = position & 0xF;  // Lower 4 bits\n    uint64_t new_overflow = (position >> DENSE_BUCKET_SIZE) & 0x7;  // Upper 3 bits\n\n    dense_bucket_[bucket_idx] = std::bitset<DENSE_BUCKET_SIZE>(new_dense);\n    if (new_overflow > 0) {\n      overflow_bucket_[bucket_idx] = std::bitset<OVERFLOW_BUCKET_SIZE>(new_overflow);\n    }\n  }\n}\n\n/** @brief Function to compute cardinality. */\ntemplate <typename T>\nauto HyperLogLogPresto<T>::ComputeCardinality() -> void {\n  if (n_leading_bits_ < 0 || dense_bucket_.empty()) {\n    cardinality_ = 0;\n    return;\n  }\n\n  // Number of buckets\n  size_t m = dense_bucket_.size();\n\n  // Compute the harmonic mean: sum of 2^(-M[j])\n  double sum = 0.0;\n  for (size_t j = 0; j < m; j++) {\n    uint64_t dense_val = dense_bucket_[j].to_ullong();\n    uint64_t overflow_val = 0;\n    if (overflow_bucket_.find(static_cast<uint16_t>(j)) != overflow_bucket_.end()) {\n      overflow_val = overflow_bucket_[static_cast<uint16_t>(j)].to_ullong();\n    }\n    uint64_t register_val = (overflow_val << DENSE_BUCKET_SIZE) | dense_val;\n    sum += std::pow(2.0, -static_cast<double>(register_val));\n  }\n\n  // Cardinality = CONSTANT * m^2 / sum\n  double raw_estimate = CONSTANT * static_cast<double>(m) * static_cast<double>(m) / sum;\n\n  cardinality_ = static_cast<uint64_t>(raw_estimate);\n}\n\ntemplate class HyperLogLogPresto<int64_t>;\ntemplate class HyperLogLogPresto<std::string>;\n}  // namespace bustub\n"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":0,"cache_creation_input_tokens":3335,"cache_read_input_tokens":57137,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":3335,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":5,"service_tier":"standard"},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"4b8426ba-0733-4a97-add4-4f00343c33e5","uuid":"2c76cc05-6d9d-42a6-851f-5035f1ff13f0"}

 // Mark as invalid\n    dense_bucket_.resize(0);\n  } else {\n    // Number of buckets is 2^n_leading_bits\n    size_t num_buckets = 1ULL << n_leading_bits_;\n    dense_bucket_.resize(num_buckets, std::bitset<DENSE_BUCKET_SIZE>(0));\n  }\n}\n\n/** @brief Element is added for HLL calculation. */\ntemplate <typename KeyType>\nauto HyperLogLogPresto<KeyType>::AddElem(KeyType val) -> void {\n  if (n_leading_bits_ < 0) {\n    return;\n  }\n\n  std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);\n\n  // Get hash value\n  hash_t hash = CalculateHash(val);\n\n  // Extract bucket index from the FIRST n_leading_bits (MSB side)\n  // For a 64-bit hash, the top bits determine the bucket\n  uint16_t bucket_idx = 0;\n  if (n_leading_bits_ > 0) {\n    bucket_idx = static_cast<uint16_t>(hash >> (64 - n_leading_bits_));\n  }\n\n  // Calculate position of leftmost 1 in the remaining bits\n  // Clear the top n_leading_bits_ from the hash\n  hash_t remaining;\n  if (n_leading_bits_ >= 64) {\n    remaining = 0;\n  } else {\n    // Mask out the top n_leading_bits_ bits\n    if (n_leading_bits_ == 0) {\n      remaining = hash;\n    } else {\n      remaining = hash << n_leading_bits_;\n    }\n  }\n\n  // Count leading zeros + 1 to get position of leftmost 1\n  uint64_t position;\n  if (remaining == 0) {\n    // All zeros, position is at the end\n    position = 64 - n_leading_bits_ + 1;\n  } else {\n    // Find position of leftmost 1 (1-indexed)\n    position = 1;\n    for (int i = 63; i >= 0; i--) {\n      if ((remaining >> i) & 1) {\n        break;\n      }\n      position++;\n    }\n  }\n\n  // Now store this value using dense_bucket_ (4 bits) and overflow_bucket_ (3 bits)\n  // Total 7 bits can represent values 0-127\n  // Dense bucket stores lower 4 bits\n  // Overflow bucket stores upper 3 bits\n\n  // Current value in registers\n  uint64_t current_dense = dense_bucket_[bucket_idx].to_ullong();\n  uint64_t current_overflow = 0;\n  if (overflow_bucket_.find(bucket_idx) != overflow_bucket_.end()) {\n    current_overflow = overflow_bucket_[bucket_idx].to_ullong();\n  }\n  uint64_t current_value = (current_overflow << DENSE_BUCKET_SIZE) | current_dense;\n\n  // Update if new position is larger\n  if (position > current_value) {\n    // Store new value\n    uint64_t new_dense = position & 0xF;  // Lower 4 bits\n    uint64_t new_overflow = (position >> DENSE_BUCKET_SIZE) & 0x7;  // Upper 3 bits\n\n    dense_bucket_[bucket_idx] = std::bitset<DENSE_BUCKET_SIZE>(new_dense);\n    if (new_overflow > 0) {\n      overflow_bucket_[bucket_idx] = std::bitset<OVERFLOW_BUCKET_SIZE>(new_overflow);\n    }\n  }\n}\n\n/** @brief Function to compute cardinality. */\ntemplate <typename T>\nauto HyperLogLogPresto<T>::ComputeCardinality() -> void {\n  if (n_leading_bits_ < 0 || dense_bucket_.empty()) {\n    cardinality_ = 0;\n    return;\n  }\n\n  // Number of buckets\n  size_t m = dense_bucket_.size();\n\n  // Compute the harmonic mean: sum of 2^(-M[j])\n  double sum = 0.0;\n  for (size_t j = 0; j < m; j++) {\n    uint64_t dense_val = dense_bucket_[j].to_ullong();\n    uint64_t overflow_val = 0;\n    if (overflow_bucket_.find(static_cast<uint16_t>(j)) != overflow_bucket_.end()) {\n      overflow_val = overflow_bucket_[static_cast<uint16_t>(j)].to_ullong();\n    }\n    uint64_t register_val = (overflow_val << DENSE_BUCKET_SIZE) | dense_val;\n    sum += std::pow(2.0, -static_cast<double>(register_val));\n  }\n\n  // Cardinality = CONSTANT * m^2 / sum\n  double raw_estimate = CONSTANT * static_cast<double>(m) * static_cast<double>(m) / sum;\n\n  cardinality_ = static_cast<uint64_t>(raw_estimate);\n}\n\ntemplate class HyperLogLogPresto<int64_t>;\ntemplate class HyperLogLogPresto<std::string>;\n}  // namespace bustub\n","numLines":139,"startLine":1,"totalLines":139}}}
